5215: ------------------------------------------------------|
5217: tad anena prakāreṇa marmābhighātakṣatān maraṇam, na tv amarmābhighātakṣatāt|
5219: iti śrīvaidyapatisiṃhaguptasūnuśrīmadvāgbhaṭaviracitā yām aṣṭāṅgahṛdayasaṃhitāyāṃ dvitīye śārīrasthāne marmavibhāgo nāma caturtho 'dhyāyaḥ||4||
5221: athāto garbhavyāpadaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ|
5221: iti ha smāhur ātreyādayo maharṣayaḥ||1||
5223: strī ced āpannagarbhā parihāryyāṇy āseveta|
5223: tataś ca yasyāḥ bastipārśvaśroṇiyonimukheṣu śūlaṃ puṣpadarśanaṃ ca syāt tāṃ mṛdusukhaśiśirāstaraṇaśayanasthām īṣadavanataśirasaṃ śītapradehapariṣekādibhir upācaret||2||
5225: tad yathā-sahasradhautena sarpiṣā'dhonābheḥ sarvataḥ pradihyāt|
5225: madhukasiddhasarpiṣā suśītena picum āplāvya yonisamīpe sthāpayet|
5225: gavyena payasā madhukāmbunā nyagrodhādikaṣāyeṇa va pariṣecayet|
5225: paripītatatsvarasāni ca tailāni yonau nidhāpayet|
5225: tacchṛṅgasiddhasya vā kṣīrasarpiṣaḥ picum||3||
5227: atisravati tu rakte tata eva akṣamātraṃ prāśayet|
5227: keva- lād eva vā kṣīrasarpiṣaḥ|
5227: nyagrodhā- ditvakpravālakalkaṃ vā|
5227: śaśaiṇahariṇarudhiraṃ vā sakṣaudraṃ pāyayet|
5227: ṛddhividārījīvantīr vā payasā|
5227: nīlotpala- sitāpakvaloṣṭhacandanādi vā|
5227: taṇḍulāmbuvāsārasānya- tareṇa|
5227: gandhapriyaṅgusitotpalaśālūkodumbaraśalāṭunya- grodhaśṛṅgāni vā''jena payasā|
5227: kaśerukamṛṇālakaśṛṅgāṭakakāśmaryaparuṣakadrākṣāsāri- vāprapauṇḍarīkānantotpalakadambabījakvāthena vā śarka- rāmadhumadhureṇa śālicūrṇam|
5227: jīvanīyaśṛtaṃ vā kṣīram|
5227: balātibalāśāliṣaṣṭikekṣumūlakākolīśṛtena payasā samadhuśarkareṇa raktaśālyodanaṃ suśītaṃ bhojayet|
5227: śīta- vīryyajāṅgalarasena vā|
5227: raktapittakriyāś ca śodhanavarjyāḥ prayuñjīta|
5227: saumyāś ca mano 'nukūlāḥ kathāḥ||4||
5229: adṛṣṭaśoṇitāṃ tu madhukapayasyāsuradārubhiḥ siddhaṃ payaḥ pāyayet|
5229: vidāryyādigaṇena vā|
5229: śvadaṃṣṭrāsvarasakṣīrasiddhaṃ vā sarpiḥ|
5229: palāṇḍurasasiddhaṃ vā sakṣaudram||5||
5231: asampūrṇatrimāsāyās tu puṣpadarśane garbhaḥ prāyo na tiṣṭha- ty asañjātasāratvāt||6||
5233: āmānvaye cordhvam api viruddhopakramatvāt|
5233: tatra rūkṣaśī- tāni prayuñjīta||7||
5235: tad yathā-prāg upavāsaṃ tato durālabhāmṛtośīraparpaṭaka- ghanacandanātiviṣābalākaṭvaṅgadhānyākakvāthapānam|
5235: tṛṇadhānyaśāliṣaṣṭikapeyām aśanaṃ ca mudgādiyūṣaiḥ|
5235: jite tv āme snigdhaśītāni pūrvavat||8||
5237: āmagarbhe tu patite madyānām anyatamaṃ sāmarthyataḥ pāyayet|
5237: garbhakoṣṭhaviśuddhyartham artivismaraṇārthañ ca|
5237: amadyapāṃ pāca- nīyadravyopahitābhiḥ snehalavaṇavarjyābhiḥ satilābhi- ruddālakādiyavāgūbhir upācared yāvanto māsās tāvantya- hānīti|
5237: tataḥ prīṇanair balānurakṣibhir asnehair āhārair ādoṣadhātukleda- śuddheḥ|
5237: āmagarbhaśeṣeṇa hi punaḥ punar akuśalam anuṣajyeta|
5237: tasmāt tīkṣṇair anavaśeṣayann upācaret|
5237: tataḥ paraṃ snehapānai- rbastibhir āhāraiś ca dīpanīyapācanīyajīvanīyabṛṃhaṇīya- madhuravātaharair iti||9||
5239: yasyāḥ punar mahati jātasāre garbhe varjyānām avarjanāt puṣpa- darśanaṃ syād anyad vā yonisravaṇam|
5239: tasyās tannimittaṃ vāyuḥ prakupitaḥ pittaśleṣmāṇau parigṛhya garbhasya rasavahāṃ nāḍīṃ pratipīḍyāvatiṣṭhate|
5239: tato nāḍyāṃ doṣaiḥ kulyāyām iva vahanād garbho vṛddhim anāpnuvann upaviśaty upaśuṣyati vā||10||
5243: kukṣiś ca vṛddho 'pi parihīyate|
5243: tad upaśuṣkakaṃ nāgodaraṃ ca||12||
5245: tau tu mātur āhāratejasā'lpenāpyāyyamānau yadā puṣṭau syātāṃ keśadaśanādiyuktau|
5245: tadā varṣagaṇair api pramadā prasūyeta eva||13||
5247: tatra vātenopaviṣṭakopaśuṣkakayor vāyuḥ pratihanyate|
5247: saśabdaṃ phenilaṃ vicchinnaṃ śakṛd upaveśyate|
5247: mūtram uparudhyate|
5247: kaṭīpṛṣṭhahṛdayeṣu vedanā|
5247: jṛmbhā, nidrānāśo 'bhīkṣṇaṃ pratiśyāyaḥ, śuṣkakāsaḥ, sādaḥ, kṣveḍete iva karṇau, tudyete iva śaṅkhau|
5247: pipīlikābhir iva saṃsṛjyate śarīraṃ pariṛntann iva vāyur bhramati kukṣau tama iva praveśyate|
5247: duḥkhenānnasya jaraṇam|
5247: sphuṭitavivarṇaparuṣatvaktvaṃ ca bhavati||14||
5249: pittena tāmraharitam upaveśyate|
5249: dhūmako 'mlakaś chardimūrcchākṣihṛdayadāhaḥ|
5249: pītaraktagomūtrābhanetramūtranakhatvaktvaṃ kālī durbalā nityaśūnā ca nārī bhavati||15||
5251: śleṣmaṇā madhurāsyatvam utkleśaḥ śleṣmodvamanaṃ bhaktadveṣaḥ śvetahastapādanetratā kāsaḥ śvāsaś ca||16||
5253: tayor jīvanīyabṛṃhaṇīyamadhurauṣasiddhānāṃ sarpiṣām upayogaḥ|
5253: tathā payasāṃ rasānām āmagarbhāṇāṃ ca garbhavṛddhikaraḥ|
5253: tathā sambhojanam etair eva ca ghṛtādibhiḥ|
5253: subhikṣāyā abhīkṣṇaṃ yānavāhanāpavarjjanabṛṃhaṇair upapādanam||17||
5255: nivātaṃ ca seveta||18||
5257: pitte madhukavidārīniryyūhasiddhaṃ payaḥ pibet|
5257: tisraḥ sthūlakukkuṭīr nistuṣamāṣāḍhakaṃ ca kṣīrodakenolūkhale saṃkṣudya tadrasena sarpiṣā ca tittirikapiñjalānyatararasaṃ sādhayitvā pāyayed upaviṣṭakavivardhanārtham upaśuṣkakabṛṃhaṇārthaṃ ca|
5257: ajāsarpiṣā kṣīreṇa jīvanīyaiś ca saṃskṛtāṃ yavāgūṃ pibet|
5257: payasyā kākolīdvayasuniṣaṇḍakakalkena payasā ca sarpir vipācayet|
5257: tat kālyaṃ kṣīrānupānam upayuñjīta|
5257: āma- garbharasena cāśnīyāt|
5257: tān eva ca tatra bhṛṣṭān bhakṣayet|
5257: jalacaramāṃsāni ca||19||
5259: kaphe tv ānūpaudakapiśitopadaṃśām acchasurāṃ trirātraṃ pañcarātraṃ saptarātraṃ vā pibet|
5259: tilamudgamāṣalavaṇabilvapātraiḥ|
5259: pañcabhir ghṛtapātraṃ saṃyojya svanuguptaṃ nidhāpayet|
5259: tataḥ prātaḥ piṇḍaṃ bilvamātraṃ śṛtaśītacchāgadugdhanupānaṃ vā'śnīyāt|
5259: atraiva vā badara- cūrṇapātram api nidadhyāt||20||
5261: evam avṛddhau tīkṣṇair virecayed aparāpātanīyaiś ca pātayet||21||
5263: yasyāḥ punar vātopasṛṣṭasrotasi līno garbhaḥ prasupto na spandate taṃ līnam ity āhuḥ||22||
5265: tatra śyenotkrośagomatsyagodhāśikhikukkuṭatittirīṇām anyatamasya rasena bhūrisarpiṣkeṇa māṣayūṣeṇa vā ghṛtāḍhyena raktaśālyodanaṃ bhojayet|
5265: saktūn vā tilamāṣabilvaśalāṭuyuktān payasa pāyayet medyamāṃsopadaṃśaṃ vā mārdvīkam|
5265: tailena cābhīkṣṇam udaravaṅkṣaṇorukaṭīpārśvapṛṣṭhāny abhyajyāt|
5265: muhur muhuś caināṃ harṣayet|
5265: pūrvoktaṃ cekṣeta||23||
5267: yasyāḥ punar udāvartavibandhaḥ syāt tāṃ vātaharasnigdhānnapānair upācaret|
5267: aṣṭame tu māse madhukasiddhena tailenānuvāsayet|
5267: tadasiddhau vīraṇaśālikuśakāśekṣubālikāvetasaparivyādhamūlānāṃ bhūtīkānantākāśmaryaparūṣakamadhūkamadhukamṛdvīkānāṃ ca payasā'rdhodakenodgṛhya rasaṃ tena priyālabibhītakamajjatilakalkayukteneṣallavaṇoṣṇena nirūhayet|
5269: udāvarto hi samupekṣitaḥ sahasā sagarbhāṃ garbhiṇīm atipātayet||25||
5271: yasyāḥ punar atimātradoṣopacayād yathoktair vā vyavāyādibhir anyair vā vyādhibhiḥ pūrvopacitena vā jananyapatyayoḥ karmaṇā bandhanān mucyate garbhaḥ phalam iva vṛntāt samuktabandhano garbhaśayyām atikramya kṛtaplīhāntravivarair avasraṃsamānaḥ koṣṭhasaṃkṣobham āpādayati|
5271: tataḥ koṣṭhasaṃkṣobhād vāyur apāno mūḍhaḥ kukṣibastipārśvodarayoniśūlānāhaviṇmūtrasaṅgān āpādya garbhaṃ cyāvayati taruṇaṃ śoṇitabhāvena|
5271: so 'bhihitaḥ prāk||26||
5273: vivṛddhe tu garbhe stabdhaṃ stimitaṃ śītam aśmagarbham ivodaram ābhāti|
5273: śūlam adhikam upajāyate|
5273: na ca spandate garbho nā''vyaḥ prādurbhavanti|
5273: na sravati yonir akṣiṇī cāsyāḥ sraṃsyete|
5273: tathā bhṛśam aratiparītā vyathate 'nyathā ceṣṭate tāmyati bhrāmyati rodity aharniśam|
5273: na svapiti|
5273: pūtyucchvāsā kṛcchrāc chvasity atikaṣṭaṃ prāṇiti|
5273: jakṣitīty evaṃvidhāṃ striyaṃ mṛtagarbhāṃ vidyāt||27||
5275: taṃ tu garbhaṃ kadācid asamyag apatyapatham anekadhā pratipannaṃ viguṇeneva vāyunā pīḍitaṃ mohitaṃ ca mūḍhagarbham ity āhuḥ|
5275: viguṇānilapratipīḍanavaicitryād asaṅkhyagatiṃ ca||28||
5277: samāsatas tu trividhā gatir urdhvā, tiryyaṅ, nyubjā ca|
5277: saṃsthānāni punar aṣṭau|
5277: tāny atraivottaratra vakṣyante||29||
5279: teṣu sarveṣv api viparītendriyārthām ākṣepayonibhraṃśasaṃvaraṇamakkalaśvāsabhramābhipannāṃ śītagātrāṃ pūtyudgārāṃ ca varjayet||30||
5281: na tu kṣaṇam apy upekṣeta garbhaśalyam|
5281: tad vinirucchavāsīkṛtyā''śu jananīṃ hanyāt||31||
5283: sacetanaś ca garbhaḥ śastreṇa vidāryyamāṇo viṣayam aṅgāni vikṣipet|
5283: tasmāt tatsaṅge sūtyaḥ prayateran purvoktāni ca mantrauṣadhāni prayuñjīta||32||
5285: trividhas tu saṅgo bhavati|
5285: śirasy aṃse jaghane vā|
5285: tasya hi saṅgas trividho bhavati|
5285: mūḍhagarbhanirharaṇan tatra matāntarāṇi mūḍhagarbhasya tu jarāyupātanasāmānyaṃ karmety eke|
5285: mantrādika- rmātharvavedavihitam ity eke|
5285: dṛṣṭakarmaṇā śalyahartrā śalyā- haraṇam ity eke||33||
5287: na tv ataḥ kaṣṭatamam asti śastrakarma|
5287: yato yoniyakṛtplīhāntragarbhāśayādīnāṃ madhye tān anupahiṃsatā'sparśavatā śastram avacāryyam utkarṣaṇavyāvartanotkartanacchedanabhedanapīḍanarjūkaraṇāni caikena pāṇinā kāryyāṇi||34||
5289: tathodarā'śmarīprabhṛtīn api||35||
5291: athottānāyā vyābhugnasakthyā vastracumbalān namitakaṭipradeśāyāś ca dhanvanāgavṛntikāśālmalīpicchāghṛtair abhyajya hastaṃ yoniṃ ca garbham āharet||36||
5293: tatra sakthibhyām āgatasyānulomam āñchanam eva|
5293: ekasakthiprapannasyetaraṃ sakthiṃ prasāryya|
5293: sphigdeśāgatasya sphigdeśaṃ pratiṣidhyordhvam utkṣipya sakthinī prasāryya|
5293: tiryyag āgatasya parighasyevāparārdham utkṣipya pūrvārdham apatyapathaṃ pratyārjavam ānīya|
5293: pārśvāpavṛttaśirasoṃ'saṃ pratipīḍyordhvam utkṣipya śiro 'patyapatham ānīya|
5293: bāhudvayaprapannasyordhvam utpīḍyā'sau śiromūlam ānīya nirharet||37||
5295: yas tv ābhugnamadhyaḥ pāṇipādaśirobhir yoniṃ pratipadyate|
5295: yaś cābhugna ekena pādena yoniṃ dvitīyena pāyum|
5295: tau mūḍhau hastenāhartum aśakyāv iti śastram avacārayet||38||
5297: atha striyam āśvāsya maṇḍalāgreṇāṅguliśastreṇa vā (śiro) vidāryya śirāḥ kapālāny āhṛtya garbhaśaṅkunā gṛhītvā cibuke tāluny urasi kakṣyāyāṃ vā'paharet|
5297: abhinnaśirasaṃ cākṣikūṭe gaṇḍe vā|
5297: tathāsaṃsaktasyāṃsadeśe bāhuṃ chitvā|
5301: cūrṇaṃ snehena kalkaṃ vā kvāthaṃ vā pāyayettaḥ|
5303: atha balātailam upadekṣyāmaḥ|
5303: balāmūlākaṣāyasya bhāgāḥ ṣaṭ|
5303: tāvanta eva payaso yavakolakulatthadaśamūlaka- ṣāyasya bhāgo bhāgaś caturdaśas tailasya|
5303: eṣa bhagavato dhanvantare- rabhimatas tailarājo rājñāṃ rājamātrāṇāṃ sūtikānāṃ kṛśānāṃ śiśūnām atītavayasāṃ sukumārāṇāṃ śūlināṃ mahāgraha- gṛhītānāṃ dīrgharogakṣapitavapuṣāṃ marmaviddhapatitābhihata- bhagnaviśliṣṭadehānāṃ daivopaghātātabhiśaptamanojvaraparī- tānāṃ pānanāvanābhyaṅgabastiṣu prayojyaḥ|
5303: tena bala- pauruṣasmṛtimatimedhāgnivīryyaujāṃsi vivardhante|
5303: pakṣā- ghātākṣepakārdditādivividhavātavikāragulmahikkāśvāsa- kāsā nivartante|
5303: ahar ahaś copayujyamāne ṣaṇmāsād antra- vṛddhiḥ praśāmyati ca|
5315: athāto vikṛtivijñānīyaṃ śārīraṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ|
5315: iti ha smāhur ātreyādayo maharṣayaḥ|

5523: maraṇaṃ prāṇināṃ dṛṣṭam āyuḥpuṇyobhakṣayāt|
5523: iti śrī vaidyapatisiṃhaguptasūnuśrīmadbāgbhāṭaviracitā- yām aṣṭāṅgahṛdayasaṃhitāyāṃ dvitīye śārīrasthāne vikṛtivijñānīyo nāma pañcamo 'dhyāyaḥ||5||
5525: athāto 'ṅgavibhāgaṃ śārīraṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ|
5525: iti ha smāhur ātreyādayo maharṣayaḥ||1||
5527: sakalam api śarīram aṅgam ity ucyate|
5527: tatra punaḥ ṣaḍaṅgam|
5527: śire 'ntarādhir dvau bāhū sakthinī ca|
5527: netranābhipāṇipādā- dīni tv asya pratyaṅgāni||2||
5529: mahāguṇamayebhyaś ca khapavanatejojalabhūmyākhyebhyo mahābhūtebhyaś cetanādhiṣṭhitebhyo 'bhinirvṛttir aṅgasya||3||
5531: bhūtānām eva ca dṛṣṭādṛṣṭavividhakarmavaśād anekarūpāt sanniveśaṣadākṛtipramāṇasnehadīptisvarādīnāṃ sārupyam asārūpyaṃ vā sūkṣmasthūlatāratamyabhedabhinnam atibahuprakāraṃ niṣpadyate||4||
5533: tatra sattbahulam ākaśam||5||
5535: rajobahulo vāyuḥ|1|
5537: sattvatamobahulam ambu|1|
5539: tamobahulā bhūḥ||1||
5539: teṣām apratighātaś calatvam auṣṇyaṃ dravatā kāṭhinyam iti kramā- lliṅgāni||6||
5541: viśeṣataś ca śrotrādiṣv indriyeṣv avasthānam||7||
5543: śabdasparśarūparasagandhair ekaikapravṛddhair anvayaḥ||8||
5545: tatrā''kāśajāni śrotraṃ śabdaḥ sarvastrotāṃsi viviktatā||9||
5547: vāyavīyāni sparśanaṃ sparśaḥ praśvāsocchvāsādiparispandanāni lāghavaṃ ca||10||
5549: āgneyāni darśanaṃ rūpaṃ pittam ūṣmā paktiḥ santāpo medhā varṇo bhās tejaḥ śauryyañ ca||11||
5551: āmbhasāni rasanaṃ rasaḥ svedakledavasā'sṛkaśukramūtrādidravasamūhaḥ śaityaṃ snehaś ca||12||
5553: pārthīvāni ghrāṇaṃ gandhaḥ keśanakhāsthyādimūrtasamūho dhairyyasthairyyañ ca||13||
5555: teśu mātṛjāni mṛdūni tvagraktamāṃsamedomajjanābhihṛdayāmāśayagarbhāśayayakṛtplīhāklomāntragudādīni||14||
5557: pitṛjāni sthirāṇi keśanakhāsthiśukrasirāsnāyvādīni||15||
5559: ātmajāni nānāyoniṣūtpittir manaś cetanendriyāṇi prāṇāpānau dhāraṇam ākṛtisvaravarṇaviśeṣāḥ kāmakrodhalobhabhayaharṣadharmaśīlatāsmṛtibuddhīcchādveṣaprayatnāhaṅkārasukhaduḥkhāyurātmajñānāni ca||16||
5561: sātmyajāny āyurārogyam anālāsyalolupatvam indriyaprasādasvaravarṇaujaḥ sampatpraśastatā praharṣabhūyastvaṃ medhābalaṃ ca||17||
5563: rasajāni kṛtsnasya dehasya sambhavo vṛttir vṛddhis tṛptir alaulyaṃ puṣṭir utsāhaś ca||18||
5565: śuddhasattvajāni śaucam āstikatvaṃ, kṛtajñatā, dākṣiṇyaṃ, vyavasāyaḥ, śauryyaṃ, gāmbhiryaṃ buddhir medhā, smṛtiḥ śuklavartmarucir bhaktir abhiṣaṅgābhāvas tamoguṇo viparyayaś ca||19||
5567: rājasāni durupacāratā'nāryaṃ śauryyaṃ mātsaryam amita- bhāṣitvam ahaṅkāro lolupatvaṃ dambho mānaḥ krodho harṣaḥ kāmaś ca||20||
5569: tāmasāny ajñānaṃ viṣādaḥ pramādo nidrā''lasyaṃ kṣuttṛṣṇā śoko mātsaryaṃ vipratipattiḥ parātisandhānaṃ sattvaguṇa vaiparītyaṃ ca||21||
5571: tatra sattvaṃ manas tasyopaplavo rajastamaś ca ity evambhūtātmakaṃ deham āhuḥ||22||
5573: tasya punaḥ pravibhāgaḥ-tvacaḥ kalā doṣā dhātavo malā buddhīndriyāṇi tadadhiṣṭhitāni karmendriyāṇy atīndriyam āśayāḥ prāṇāyatanāni kaṇḍarājālāni kūrcā rajjvas sīvanyo 'sthisaṅghātāḥ sīmantā asthīny asthisandhayaḥ snāyūni peśyaḥ sirā dhamanyaḥ srotāṃsy ūṣmāṇo marmaṇi keśaśmaśrulomāni prakṛtivikṛtibhedāś ca||23||
5575: tatrā'sṛjaḥ pacyamānasya kṣīrasyeva santānikāḥ ṣaṭ tvaco bhavanti||24||
5577: tvakprasādād raktasya prasādaḥ|
5577: udakadharā tvak tāsām udakadharā cādyā||25||
5579: dvitīyā'sṛgdharā||26||
5581: tṛtīyā sidhmakilāsādhiṣṭhānā||27||
5583: pañcamī punar alajīvidradhyadhiṣṭhānā|
5583: prāṇadharā tvak ṣaṣṭhī tu prāṇadhara|
5583: yasyāṃ chinnāyāṃ tāmyaty andham iva ca tamaḥ praviśati yāṃ cādhiṣṭhāyārūṃṣi jāyante|
5583: parvasu kṛṣṇaraktāni sthūlamūlāni duścikitsyatamāni||28||
5585: anye sapta tvaco vadanti|
5585: bhāsinī tvak tatra prathamā bhāsinī nāma yā varṇānām avabhāsinī khādīnāṃ ca pañcānāṃ chāyānāṃ sā vrīher aṣṭādaśabhāgapramāṇā||29||
5587: dvitīyā lohinī nāma ṣoḍaśabhāgapramāṇā|1|
5587: tṛtīyā śvetā nāma dvādaśabhāgapramāṇā|1|
5587: caturthī tāmrā nāmā'ṣṭabhāgapramāṇā|1|
5587: pañcamī vedinī nāma pāñcabhāgapramāṇā|1|
5587: ṣaṣṭhī rohiṇī nāma vrīhipramāṇā|1|
5587: yas tu dhātvāśayāntareṣu kledo 'vatiṣṭhate sa yathā svam ūṣmabhir vipakvaḥ snāyuśleṣmajarāyucchannaḥ kāṣṭha iva sāro dhātusāraśeṣo rasaśeṣolpatvāt kalāsaṃjñaḥ||30||
5589: tā dhātvāśayantaramaryādāḥ sapta kalāḥ||1||
5589: tāsāṃ prathamā māṃsadharā nāma|
5589: yasyāṃ māṃse sirāsnāyudhamanīsrotasāṃ bhūmāv iva paṅkodakena bisamṛṇālānāṃ pratānāni bhavanti||31||
5591: dvitīyā raktadhara nāma māṃsasyābhyantaratas tasyāṃ śoṇitaṃ viśeṣataś ca sirāplīhayakṛtsu bhavati|
5593: tṛtīyā medodharā nāma|
5595: caturthī śleṣmadharā nāma|
5595: tatsthena hi śleṣmaṇā śleṣitāḥ sarvasandhayo dṛḍhā bhavanti sopāṅgā sābhyaṅgā||34||
5597: pañcamī purīṣadharā nāma|
5597: sā hy antrāmapakvāśayāśritā koṣṭhāntaruṇḍukasthaṃ malaṃ vibhajati||35||
5599: ṣaṣṭhī pittadhara nāma pakvāmāśamadhyasthā|
5599: sā hy antaragner adhiṣṭhānatayā''māśayāt pakvāśayonmukham annaṃ balena vidhārya pittatejasa śoṣayati pacati pakvañ ca vimuñcati|
5599: doṣādhiṣṭhitā tu daurbalyād āmam evātato 'sāv annasya grahaṇāt punar grahaṇīsaṃjñā|
5599: balaṃ ca tasyāḥ pittam evāgnyabhidhānam ataḥ sā'gnistabdhopabṛṃhitaikayogakṣemā śarīraṃ vartayati||36||
5601: saptamī śukradharā nāma dvyaṅgule dakṣiṇe pārśve bastidvārasyādho mūtramārgam āśritā sakalaśarīravyāpinī śukraṃ pravartayati||37||
5603: doṣā dhātavo malāś ca prāguditāḥ||38||
5605: pañca buddhīndriyāṇi-śrotraṃ sparśanaṃ darśanaṃ rasanaṃ ghrāṇañ ca|
5605: śrotrādīni pañca buddhīndriyāṇi||39||
5605: teṣāṃ sabhāgatayā kramād viṣayaḥ śabdasparśarūparasagandhāḥ||1||
5613: buddhikarmendriyobhayātmakatvāc ca||44||
5615: saptāśayāḥ kramād asṛkkaphāmapittapakvavāyumūtrādhārāḥ|

5761: anyāni ca trīṇi strīṇāṃ stanau raktapathaś ca||19||
5763: tathā'parāṇy antassrotāṃsi jīvitāyatanāni trayodaśa prāṇodakānnadhātumalānām ayanāni||20||
5765: tatra prāṇavāhināṃ hṛdayaṃ mūlaṃ mahāsrotaś ca||21||
5767: tāni dhātukṣayaraukṣyapipāsākṣudvyāyāmavegadhāraṇādibhir duṣyanti||22||
5769: tato 'tisṛṣṭaṃ pratibaddham alpālpam abhīkṣṇaṃ vā saśabdaśūlam ucchvasanam|
5769: tatra śvāsavat sādhanam||23||
5771: udakavāhināṃ tālu mūlaṃ kloma ca|1|
5771: tāny āmabhayātipānoṣṇaśuṣkānnakaṣāyatṛṇanigrahādibhir duṣyanti||1||
5771: tato 'titṛṣṇā mukhaśoṣaḥ karṇakṣveḍanaṃ tamodarśanañ ca|
5771: duṣṭodakavahasrotassu cikitsā||2||
5771: tatra tṛṣṇoktam auṣadham||24||
5773: annavāhinām āmāśayo mūlaṃ vāmapārśvañ ca||1||
5773: teṣāṃ duṣṭau sarvaṃ mātrāśitīyoktam avagacchet||25||
5775: rasavāhināṃ hṛdayaṃ mūlaṃ daśa dhamanyaś ca|1|
5775: raktavāhināṃ yakṛt plīhā ca|1|
5775: māṃsavāhināṃ snāyūni tvak ca|1|
5775: medovāhināṃ vṛkkau māṃsaṃ ca|1|
5775: asthivāhināṃ jaghanaṃ medaś ca|1|
5775: majjavāhināṃ parvāṇy asthīni ca|1|
5775: śukravāhināṃ stanau muṣkau majjā ca|1|
5775: mūtravāhināṃ bastir vaṅkṣaiṇā ca|1|
5775: śakṛdvāhināṃ pakvāśayaḥ sthūlāntraṃ ca|1|
5775: svedavāhināṃ medo romakūpāś ca||1||
5775: teṣāṃ dūṣaṇaṃ vividham ahitāhāravihāram upadravān pṛthak pṛthak sādhanaṃ doṣādivijñānīyād yathāsvaṃ vṛddhikṣayalakṣaṇena lakṣayet||26||
5777: vyadhe tu srotasāṃ bhramamohakampapralāpādhmānaśūlā'rucitṛṭchardijvarātirudhirasrutimūtrapurīṣarodhā maraṇañ ca||27||
5779: tasmāt srotoviddhaṃ pratyākhyāyopacaret||28||
5781: uddhṛtaśalyaṃ ca sadyovraṇavidhānena||29||
5783: tatra srotasām eva viśeṣāḥ sirādhamanyaḥ||30||
5785: apare punar āhuḥ-srotāṃsi sirādhamanyo rasavāhinyo nāḍyaḥ panthāno 'yanāni mārgāḥ śarīracchidrāṇi saṃvṛ tāsaṃvṛtāni sthānāny āśayāḥ kṣayā niketāś ceti śarīre dhātvavakāśānāṃ lakṣyālakṣyāṇāṃ nāmānīti||32||
5785: te cāvakāśāḥ prakupitāḥ sthānasthān mārgasthāṃś ca dhātūn prakopayanti|
5785: te 'pi kupitās tāni srotāṃsi srotāṃsi ca srotāṃsi|
5785: dhātavaś ca dhātūn||3||
5785: teṣāṃ sarveṣām eva dūṣayitāro duṣṭā doṣāḥ|1|
5785: te ca prāyaśo duṣyanty agnidoṣāt|1|
5785: tasmāt tannimittāny āyurārogyabalopacayavarṇaujāṃsi|1|
5785: tatpratibaddhāny eva ca rañjakādīni bhūtāgnayo dhātvagnayaś ca||1||
5785: tathā'nnam api pittenaiva pakvam amṛtatāṃ yāty apakvaṃ ca viṣatām||33||
5787: tatra pittaviśeṣaḥ pācakasaṃjño 'gnir iti prāg abhihitaḥ||1||
5789: anye punar ācakṣate-doṣadhātumalasannipātajanito 'ntaruṣmā yathānirdiṣṭādhiṣṭhānakarmāgnir iti||35||
5791: sa khalu vidhivad abhyavahṛtam annajātaṃ prāṇena vāyunā koṣṭham ākṛṣṭaṃ dravair vibhinnasaṅghātaṃ snehena mṛdūkṛtam abhisandhukṣitaḥ samānenāmāśayasthaṃ sthālīstham ivāmbutaṇḍulam agnir antaragniḥ pacati||36||
5793: tat tu sarvarasam api pacyamānam ādau madhurībhūtaṃ kaphaṃ phenībhūtam udīrayati|
5793: tato vidagdham amlībhūtam āmāśayāt cyavamānam acchaṃ pittam|
5793: prāpya ca pakvāśayaṃ kaṭukībhūtam anilam||37||
5795: tataś caivaṃ viklinnaṃ āhāre pañca pañcātmakā mahābhūtāgnayo vāyunā vyastān yathāsvaṃ pañcaiva bhūtaguṇān āhārasthān pacanti|
5795: te pakvāḥ punar yathāsvam eva dehāśritāṃś ca svavikārabhūtān bhūtaguṇān āpyāyayanti||38||
5797: evañ ca pakvād āhārād vividhauṣadhagarbhād iva snehād acchaḥ sārabhūto rasākhyaḥ kiṭṭākhyaś ca malo 'bhinirvartate||39||
5799: tābhyāñ ca sāramalābhyāṃ tadātmakānām eva śarīraguṇānāṃ dhātvākhyānāṃ yathāsvaṃ srotāṃsi pāramparyyeṇāvicchinnasantānam āpūryyante||40||
5801: srotobhyaś ca yathāvibhāgaṃ yathāyatham eva dhātavaḥ puṣyanti||1||
5803: uttarottarānupraveśe 'pi ca purveṣāṃ srotasāṃ yathākālaṃ samyagāhāropayogena pariṇāmavatāpyāyyamānānāṃ nāpacayo bhavati|
5803: tataś ca dhātvākhyāḥ prasādamalāḥ svaṃ svam eva mānam anuvartante yathāvayaḥśarīram||42||
5805: tatrā''hāraraso vyānavikṣipto yathāsvaṃ saptasu dhātvagniṣu kramāt pacyamānaḥ svātmabhāvapracyutisamanantaram eva prāptaraktādidhātusaṃjñakaḥ kālavad askhalitabalapramāṇo deham ūrjayitvā dhātūn dhātumalāṃś ca puṣṇāti||43||
5807: athānnakiṭṭam acchaṃ mūtraṃ ghanaṃ śakṛt||44||
5809: rasasya sāro raktaṃ malaḥ kapho lasīkā ca||1||
5809: raktasya sāro māṃsaṃ kaṇḍarāḥ sirāś ca|
5809: malaḥ pittam|2|
5809: māṃsasya sāro medas tvaco vasā ca|
5809: kiṭṭaṃ karṇākṣināsikāsyaromakūpaprajananamalāḥ|2|
5809: medasaḥ sāro 'sthisnāyusandhayaḥ|
5809: kiṭṭaṃ svedaḥ|2|
5809: asthnaḥ sāro majjā|
5809: kiṭṭaṃ keśaromanakhāḥ||2||
5811: śukrasya sāram ojaḥ|
5811: atyantaśuddhatayā'sya malābhāvaḥ|
5811: anye punar ata eva tasya necchanti pākam|
5811: apare punaḥ śukrasāraṃ garbham evāmananti||46||
5813: vāyuḥ punar agner āhārasya ca bahvalpatayā tasmāt tasmān mūrcchanāviśeṣād amūrttaḥ śabdavān īṣacchabdaḥ pracuro 'lpo vā pañcātmā koṣṭhe prādurbhavati||47||
5815: evaṃ ca kṛtvā'nnamayā eva dehe sarve bhāvāḥ|
5815: tenaiva cedam evaṃ cireṇa vyāptam antarbahiḥ śarīrayantram anavaratapravāheṇa svāsthyabalopacayayuktaṃ yāvat karmopādānaṃ yāpyate|
5815: anyathā tv annarasokṣepaṇād vibaddhasrotasā vā yathāvad ana- bhisandhīyamānadhātupravāham antarā vyavacchidyate|
5815: vyādhi bhājanaṃ vā bhavati||48||
5817: anye tu varṇayanti-abhyavahṛtamātrasyāhā''rasya kaṇṭhanāḍīpraluṭhitasya mahānimnam avatīrṇasya yo ya evāṃśaḥ kāyāgninā'valīḍhaḥ pākam upanīyate tasya tasyaiva prasādākhyo rasaleśo 'bhinirvṛttisamanantaraṃ samaṃ samastadhātuṣu saṃvṛtāsaṃvṛtaiḥ pravisṛto vivṛtamukheṣv āsanneṣu srotassu bhūyān prathamataraṃ cānveti|
5817: paryyāyeṇetareṣv api|
5817: evam annarasa eva sākṣāt sarvadhātūn kenacid eva kālabhedena puṣṇāti na punar dhātavo dhātvantaratāṃ svarūpopamardena pratipadyanta iti||49||
5819: agnis tu balabhedena caturvidho bhavati|
5819: samo viṣamas tīkṣṇo mandaś ca||2||
5819: tatra samo yaḥ samyag evopayuktam annaṃ samyak pacati|1|
5819: viṣamo 'samyag apy apayuktaṃ pacati samyag api ca na pacati||1||
5819: tīkṣṇo 'samyag apy upayuktam āśu pacati|
5819: sa eva vardhamāno 'tyagnir bhavati||2||
5819: mandas tu samyag apy upayuktam udaragauravādhmānavibandhāṭopāntrakūjanamukhaśoṣavāyustambhādīny āmaliṅgāni darśayitvā cirāt pacati||1||
5819: bāhyenāpi ca jvālāṅgārātmakenāgninā naikākāraḥ sarvadā pāko bhavati|
5819: kim utāntarāśrayeṇoṣṇātmanā samenāpi|
5819: tasmāt taṃ prayatnato rakṣet||50||
5821: tatra pravareṇa sātmyena samaṃ yathāsvam apareṇetarān kramaśaḥ sātmyam āpādya pravareṇa paripālayed doṣopakramaṇiyoktena ca vātādisādhanena grahaṇīrogavihitaiś cauṣadhavyāyāmamayogair iti||51||
5837: athāto dūtādivijñānīyaṃ śārīraṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ|
5837: iti ha smāhur ātreyādayo maharṣayaḥ|
5837: gadyasūtre||2||

14639: athaiva manyase yady api tatra tantram etadguṇam, tathā'py ārṣatantrādhyayane samādhānātiśaya iti|
14641: athaivaṃ manyase vaktṛtvaviśeṣād avaśyam evārthātiśayo bhavati|
14641: tasmād ādyeṣu sukṛteṣv evābhiyogo yukto, na tv idānīntanakavikṛteṣv iti, tad etan na vācyam|
